Use whenever Arabic text is produced or checked, including "translate to Arabic", "localize", "Arabic version", and Arabic captions or slogans. Produces natural Arabic product copy written for native speakers, not literal English translations, and handles RTL layout concerns. - **arabic-natural-writing**: Write, rewrite, localize, and review Arabic so it reads as Arabic rather than English-shaped Arabic. Use for Arabic UX copy, product text, emails, captions, articles, guides, help text, notifications, localization, or any Arabic draft that feels translated, bureaucratic, stiff, or عرنجي. Diagnose foreign influence in syntax, particles, morphology, semantics, collocations, metaphors, and discourse structure; then rewrite from meaning using Arabic-native resources. Do not enforce linguistic purity: keep familiar modern terms and constructions when they are natural, precise, and useful. - **arabic-quran-hifz**: Quran Memorization (Hifz) — the systematic committing of the entire Holy Quran to memory. Covers incremental memorization methodology, Muraja'ah (revision cycles: sabaq, sabqi, manzil), Tarteel-paced recitation from memory, cross-referential recall between Surahs (mutashabihat), long-term retention strategies, and listening proficiency. A Hifdh curriculum supervisor manages both the memorization process and assessment against standard recitation accuracy. ([mediabubble-adv/mediaBubble](https://github.com/mediabubble-adv/mediaBubble/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/arabic-quran-hifz) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **arabic-quran-qiraat**: Qira'at — the ten canonical readings (Qira'at 'Ashr) of the Holy Quran, their transmission chains, textual and phonetic variations, and their relationship to tajwid, tafsir, and fiqh. Each Qira'a is a divinely revealed, mutawatir (mass-transmitted) reading traced through a chain of narrators (sanad) to the Prophet Muhammad. ([mediabubble-adv/mediaBubble](https://github.com/mediabubble-adv/mediaBubble/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/arabic-quran-qiraat) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **arabic-quran-tajwid**: Tajwid — the rules governing the correct articulation, pronunciation, and recitation of the Holy Quran. Covers Makharij al-Huruf (articulation points, all 17 across 5 anatomical regions), Sifaat al-Huruf (permanent and occasional letter attributes), Ahkam al-Madd (12 elongation rules), Ahkam an-Noon as-Sakinah wa Tanwin (4 rules), Ahkam al-Meem as-Sakinah, Qalqalah, Waqf wa Ibtida (pausing and starting), and lahn (error) correction. This skill is a prerequisite for professional Quranic recitation and is the foundation of Qira'at training. ([mediabubble-adv/mediaBubble](https://github.com/mediabubble-adv/mediaBubble/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/arabic-quran-tajwid) · ★1 · 2 copies)

Eliminates syntactic calques, westernized phraseology (العرنجية), pseudo-Arabic grammatical errors, passive-voice pollution, nominalized verb clichés, and translation loans to produce pure, idiomatic, eloquent Arabic (العربية الفصيحة الأصيلة).
